# The Wastes — completed-campaign cleanup migration manifest

**Initialized:** 2026-10-27. **Current status (2026-10-30):** The approved eleven-arc reading edition is integrated into private `main` by [merged PR #6](https://github.com/frankysan/There-Will-Be-Dungeons-Transcripts/pull/6), while all six historic word-form arcs remain unchanged. **Historical status at initialization:** Preliminary inventory and D-045 approval; implementation was then pending. Interactive browser verification remains deferred under D-078; no public deployment or visibility change is claimed. An exhaustive up-to-date file-by-file, hash-and-inbound-link census, source-claim review and migration log have **not** been performed. Based on a read-only inspection of GitHub `main` at **`c0b2a8317a1865922211920c62ae69b1a6661254`**, except where separately indicated. Do not confuse inspected paths with an accepted complete inventory.

[Campaign plan](WASTES_CAMPAIGN_CLEANUP_PLAN.md) · [D-048 reader-first presentation standard](READER_FIRST_PRESENTATION.md) · [proposed arc crosswalk](WASTES_NARRATIVE_CHAPTER_MAPS.md) · [existing world-arc manifest](WORLD_ARC_MIGRATION_MANIFEST.md) · [Delvers method](DELVERS_CLEANUP_MANIFEST.md) · [Concise change and decision history](WASTES_CLEANUP_CHANGELOG.md) · [Historical snapshot SHA/path index](REPORT_SNAPSHOT_RETENTION_INDEX.json).

## 1. Inventory method and states

**Required complete census:** all maintained files in `narrative/`, `world/` (including directories and `entities.json`), Wastes episode/navigation/control files, active `project/` policy and review files, and affected reference/report paths. Do not silently count separately scoped `delvers/` or `inverse/` content as Wastes canon. Preserve out-of-game bonuses, one-offs, early prequel and raw source inventories as distinct classes.

Future machine manifest fields per file:
- `path`, `type`, `canonical_subject_id`, `legacy_aliases`, `sha256` / blob SHA, `baseline_commit`, `source_horizon`, `last_direct_observation`.
- Page headings, prose word and paragraph counts per section, footnote/reference counts, note diagnostics, source episode IDs, inbound links and anchor references, related entities, dependencies.
- `problem_flags` (stale endpoint, research apparatus in narrative, duplicate scene/subject, unsupported identity merge, named-entity orthography, missing source, broken link, wrong classification), priority and proposed disposition.
- `archive_path`, exact preservation hash, migrated paragraph/claim trace, new canonical path or compatibility alias, review evidence and test outcome.
- Lifecycle: **not-inventoried → inventoried → outlined → archived → edited → structurally-validated → fact-reviewed → accepted**. Do not conflate a structural check with factual/audio approval. A genuine exempt register should be marked **retained-exception**, not "rewritten."

The earlier [world arc migration manifest](WORLD_ARC_MIGRATION_MANIFEST.md) records **85 world Markdown files at its 2026-10-24 baseline** and later scoped pilots; this is **not** today's number and must not be copied into the new total. The new observed subdirectories contain 46 character-related pages, 19 place pages, 32 creature pages and 21 technology pages. They include specialized registers and must be classified individually.

## 3. Domain-wide discovery leads (not certified missing entries)

| Category | High-value audit leads | Distinguishing check |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Races** | Lizardfolk, Tieflings, Lactids, Harlequinari; possible elves, dwarves, orcs, illithids, pixies | Culture/species vs individual biography vs creature encounter; no unsupported universal attributes |
| **Places** | Planet Nash, Pentel Weiss, elven Mars sites, region-labeled map locations, Solar Mines subdivisions | Standalone location vs existing place section; map inscription date/extent unverified |
| **Characters** | Badass T, omitted minor allies/adversaries, reunions, family/final status | Last **actual** appearance vs secondhand reports |
| **Factions** | Council of Earthly Defenders, Council of Faces, Umbilical Order, Arcanum Universalis | Independent organization vs office/cult vs individual; played vs unused GM designs |
| **Events / history** | Elhandar disaster, Solar Mines war, Worldbreaker banishment, Earth restoration, old dragon-war accounts | Played outcome vs NPC testimony and creator hindsight |
| **Artifacts / technology** | Important early equipment, moving items and their final holders, late spells/technical devices | Possession, demonstrated effect, claim and potential planned use kept separate |
| **Specialist registers** | Vorel's full title list; Nash's improvised casting phrases beyond E111 | In-session quotation vs retrospective memory vs non-spell poem |

Before opening a new page, search the directory, source-keyed chronology, relevant episodes and naming aliases. Record `missing`, `underindexed`, `covered in existing page`, `potential duplicate`, or `not enough evidence`.

| **Principal City** | Principle City | `world/PLACES.md` (index; dedicated page subject to evidence review) | City referred to in episodes; spelling follows owner-confirmed title, **not independently verified as a map-printed city label** | Normalize unambiguous live editorial compound; keep historical aliases and evidence classes distinct. [Audit](../reports/PRINCIPAL_RELATED_NAMES_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) |

| **Khaervilon** | Kervalon | `world/places/kervalon.md` | Owner-confirmed Known Wastes map | Main prose migration pending; preserve location ID and old path |
| **T'ietkalla** | Tietkalla, ASR alternatives | `world/places/tietkalla.md` | Known Wastes map and owner's canonical policy | Main prose migration pending; keep same place/kingdom identity |
| **Moag Ferhain** | ASR alternatives | existing chronology/name entries | Known Wastes map corroboration | Confirm exact entity locations before new page |
| **Ubere Mtaris** | ASR alternatives | existing indexes | Known Wastes map corroboration | Treat as labeled geography; scope uncertain |
| **Valiir Or'Nath** / **Stanley Billings** | historic Ornath-style surname; Stanley remains familiar name | `world/characters/stanley-billings.md` | Jon's poem and owner identity confirmation | One character with two supported names |

A label's existence on an in-world map does not prove the party visited that location, that its name was known to them earlier or that the real-world illustration may be redistributed.

**D-052 preservation/reporting rule (2026-10-29):** Preserve each document's exact working-copy baseline once before a coherent major rewrite or restructuring, and reuse it throughout subsequent batches. Incremental corrections, progress/manifest updates and read-only checks do not require full-file PRE backups. Record meaningful deltas and decisions in existing controls; create independent reports only for substantive findings. Historical files remain untouched pending a reviewed consolidation plan.

## 6. Preservation and completion tracking

For each bounded implementation batch:
1. Archive exact old bytes with hashes before any substantial rewrite and record paths in this manifest.
2. Record scene/page claims, old/new paragraphs, last-observation horizons and relevant original episode links.
3. Apply canonical names to newly authored prose and verified editorial replacements, **never** silently normalize `sources/`, historical quotations or old handout text.
4. Validate notes, links, inbound anchors and compatible slugs, compare source coverage, then run changed-file Markdownlint/diff checks as available.
5. Mark separate statuses for **structurally validated**, **fact reviewed**, **audio listened** and **accepted**; do not infer one from another.

**V — The price of succession (E062–E063):** E062 murder investigation remains unsolved despite Nash's admitted egg theft; **Zercil proposes political marriage/subordination in exchange for help against Orp; Vorel rejects**. Vorel chooses **immediate** combat before the scheduled sunrise duel. **One rocket** fired by Hope redirects under **Eye of Groxl** influence toward Vorel and explodes in crowded Cave Fengar, killing **noncombatant** Mirion, Typalla, Elder of Families, Toxar, Featherscale visitor, Mola Janda and others; **Omnom and other inhabitants survive**. Orp survives blast/fall but **Vorel kills him in E062 using the Eye's returning fire shield (not Mawstorm sword)** and **Omnom publicly recognizes Vorel as Great Fang**. Zercil **survives E062** and separately challenges Vorel E063; **Stanley discreetly heals Vorel**, Nash's dragon form distracts the crowd, **Vorel detects apparent worms and aims to incapacitate**, but **Hope kills Zercil using Scully's Obsidian Nail**. The visible intervention compromises claims of a pristine one-on-one succession. Neither event proves Zercil murdered Bramie. Stanley actually retrieves five-bead **Necklace of Fireballs** from deceased Orp; an unidentified child appears and vanishes **only before Stanley**. Vorel formally rejects feeding humans to the cyclops, but **approximately 200–300 captives are only beginning medical screening**, not all returned to freedom. Vorel/Hope capture **one living worm from Zercil's bedding**; Fengar children are alive. Stanley's separate encounter with **Follow** is uncanny but E063 does not identify him as demonic agent or worm host. Historic `ARC_THREE.md` **L27/L29**, with **L31's E063 screening clause** carried into VI's outbreak.

**VI — Worms, strangers and the storm (E064–E065):** E064 Follow **directly demonstrates telepathy** and claims a vanishing child as daughter. Guards **report** Zarks supplied Sun's Witnesses, not independently proof of sabotage. Forcible examination of a captive is inconclusive until repeated **Dollo Poppio** name triggers directly witnessed transformation and **worm spray**; Vorel burns visible worms and kills host but **no guaranteed parasite clearance**. Sun's Witnesses become **chicken lords** and other lizardfolk manifest multiple arms; Nash's fireball kills many transforming prisoners, **not all**, with ethics and fate unsettled. **Omnom alive when last seen**, takes the recovered **five-bead Necklace of Fireballs** and stays behind in Razak Val; new raptor **Mawstorm** distinct from sword. Party escapes by raptors over endangered rope bridge, camps and hears Fate 4. **Played E064 post-credit continuation** introduces Follow, girl and unnamed huge orc already driving the party's hotwired vehicle; no completed ownership handover. In E065 they identify themselves as **Follow, Sira and Rorf**; Home/massacre/origin and family relation are reported, while Sira's scarred ears and telepathy/invisibility demonstrations are observable. **Rorf admits he killed Bramie**, Follow describes strategic heir killings; that **participant's confession** is not an independent eyewitness reconstruction and **must not be backdated into E061–63**. Hope's offer of T'ietkalla sanctuary is provisionally accepted, **not achieved**. Storm floods purple desert, Hope drives and Nash raises the mired RV by earth; Stanley's Tiny Hut and Nash's trench shelter them; **Sira speaks Elvish and Stanley sings**. Returning purported future Nashes bring conflicting **egg/leg** accounts and unverified planet/time stories; **one egg retained by party and one carried away by visitor** pending original-audio custody validation. **E065 ends when a real T. rex approaches and nudges/sniffs Fate 4**; the vehicle remains intact enough to be there, **Sira, Follow and Rorf are alive**, and E066 destruction/abduction/deaths belong entirely to next chapter. Historic **L31's E064 outbreak remainder, L35 and L37's storm/egg E065 clauses** covered; **L37's E066 dinosaur destruction** reserved for VII.

**Critical source-backed chronology/knowledge boundaries for drafting:**

S2E02 **Sever** first identifies himself, crew/Delit ship-mind, first real flight, Razak Val survivors, Mars docking/christening **Fate Six**, Nash family's real reunion, Dunglestar's **incomplete** device; Mars council **opens but does not settle strategy until S2E03**.
- **II — S2E03–04:** S2E03 **Council of Earthly Defenders actually chooses Solar Mines strategy** and Stanley receives Elhandari Selle Prime appointment/transformation; proposed allies/World Breaker history are attributed where needed. S2E04 Tower Nash and multiple variants, Badass T, Planet Nash destination; do not backdate later summoning of 99 Nashes to Mars.
- **III — S2E05–06:** Planet Nash encounters, bank/teller contract/coordinates and Blast Belly Rock events; **Nash's severe mental/physical impairment after Smart/Fast Blobs persists across the S2E05 cutoff until the distinct later recovery**. Three spared orcs join Fate Six; no arrival at Pentel Weiss yet.
- **IV — S2E07–08:** Actual purple nebula transit, portal to the remote prison and warden/intake; **Badass T enters shaft at S2E07 close but is alive S2E08**, others subsequently descend. Distinguish failed spellcasting from functioning physical/magical equipment or limited class features; Tungsten is **not yet recovered** at S2E08 cutoff.
- **V — S2E09–10:** Werefolk fight and actual Badass T stabilization (left behind alive, possible lycanthropy a witness claim); **Tungsten is found and chooses to accompany them in S2E09 but is still inside prison**. S2E10 carpet fight and crypt, then **Vorel becomes psychically enthralled at the final cutoff**; no ring yet.
- **VI — S2E11–12:** Songs actually break the telepathic prison-fantasy; **one collector ring** located and carried by Nash, then transferred to Stanley in S2E12 and later to dead Nash. S2E12 actual **Nash death**, escape via Hope's Teleport with Tungsten/Union, regeneration as **conscious floating dragon-metal heart and eyestalk**, body discarded; **not full humanoid regeneration or Mars return** at the Season Two cutoff. L199 season-end status and L203 continuation note inform the boundary.
- **VII — S3E01:** Immediate continuation in nebula; Stanley actually casts **Regenerate**, Nash begins growing but full body completion is later; Hell ship boarding and battle, Squirts/Steerious/Spider evidence and actual **Principal arrival/Hand demand**; **Hand not yet seized by episode end**. Principal's claims about Tobacho and identity are not independently proven.
- **VIII — S3E02:** Principal **physically takes Hope's supernatural Hand and escapes alive**, company escapes toward Blast Belly Rock (not directly Mars); Nash's humanoid regrowth completes, **Sprout** independently appears; **Union's surgery succeeds and she remains alive**; Stanley later regenerates **Hope's ordinary organic arm** (not a replacement Hand of Voice). Doctor Spider/Steerious released alive with attached dead heads; identity/sentience of Squirts-head unverified. Fate Six resumes **Mars-bound route but does not arrive** by S3E02 cutoff.
- **IX — S3E03:** Dream of Hillmaster/Principal is **not a verified waking visit or authenticated prior-life history**, though green mind parasite is actually physically removed. Fate Six **lands on Mars**, Tungsten/Dunglestar reunite, 99 alternate Nashes are actually present, Nash rejoins Angelashi/four children; **Stanley actually gives Diplomacy to Hope**. The augmentation cylinder, weapon upgrades and rejuvenation supplies are **offered** by S3E03, but **surgeries, celebrations, finished Triangulosphere and war council belong S3E04 (Arc11)**.

**A10-R01 — APPROVAL REQUIRED / substantial pacing divergence:** The editorial guide's **350–450 words per narrative chapter is a diagnostic**, not a hard maximum. Rebalancing to the suggested diagnostic would require a meaning-changing selective condensation of scenes, agency and dialogue, and therefore **new owner approval**. Options for next gate: **accept a source-rich nine-chapter exception** as written, or separately approve a carefully staged, claim-preserving compression pass. But only **89 of those source IDs** are explicitly referenced in their respective chapter notes.
